|\chi^{5}\rangle
, restricted
by (
E.10
).

Proceeding with the above, step by step, first of all, for
l=3
, we obtain

\displaystyle b_{11}\mathcal{P}_{11}^{+}|\chi^{l}\rangle=0,\quad b_{12}\mathcal{P}_{11}^{+}\mathcal{P}_{12}^{+}|\chi^{l}\rangle=0,\quad b_{22}\mathcal{P}_{11}^{+}\mathcal{P}_{12}^{+}\mathcal{P}_{22}^{+}|\chi^{l}\rangle=0.

(E.12)

As we define the following set of operators used in
(
E.10
)–(
E.12
),

\displaystyle[\mathcal{A}^{l}]=\Bigl(b_{11}\mathcal{P}_{11}^{+},b_{12}\prod_{i}^{2}\mathcal{P}_{1i}^{+},b_{22}\mathcal{P}_{22}^{+}\prod_{i}^{2}\mathcal{P}_{1i}^{+}\Bigr),\ \ \ l=1,2,3

(E.13)

(where, for instance, the second component of the set
[\mathcal{A}^{l}]

is equal to
\mathcal{A}^{2}=b_{12}\prod_{i}^{2}\mathcal{P}_{1i}^{+}
),
we can equivalently represent the gauge conditions
(
E.10
)–(
E.12
), and all of the subsequent ones,
which are based on decomposing the gauge parameters in all
of the ghost momenta
P_{ij}^{+},i\leq j
, namely,

\displaystyle[\mathcal{A}^{l}]|\chi^{6-l}\rangle=0,\ \ \ \mathrm{for}\ \ \ l=1,2,3.

(E.14)

Next, we apply the same procedure as above, albeit starting
from the gauge parameter
|\chi^{2}\rangle
, and carry out extraction
from this parameter and from the operator
\Delta{}Q
(
E.2
)
of the ghost coordinates and momenta
\eta_{ij}
,
\mathcal{P}_{ij}^{+}
,

i\leq j
, as well as
\eta_{1}
,
\mathcal{P}_{1}^{+}
. As a result, we obtain
a set of gauge conditions for the parameter
|\chi^{2}\rangle
:

\Bigl([\mathcal{A}^{3}],b_{1}\mathcal{P}_{1}^{+}\prod_{i,j=1,i\leq j}^{2}\mathcal{P}_{ij}^{+}\Bigr)|\chi^{2}\rangle=0.

(E.15)

In the process of further extraction, starting with the ghosts
\eta_{1}
,
\eta_{2}
,

\mathcal{P}_{1}^{+}
,
\mathcal{P}_{2}^{+}
, we obtain two sets of gauge
conditions for the parameters
|\chi^{3-m}\rangle
,
m=1,2
,

\displaystyle\Bigl([\mathcal{A}^{3}],b_{1}\mathcal{P}_{1}^{+}\prod_{i,j=1,i\leq j}^{2}\mathcal{P}_{ij}^{+},b_{2}\prod_{m}^{2}\mathcal{P}_{m}^{+}\prod_{i,j=1,i\leq j}^{2}\mathcal{P}_{ij}^{+}\Bigr)|\chi^{1}\rangle=0.

(E.16)

Finally, implementing the same algorithm as the one above, albeit
initiated by the field
|\chi^{0}\rangle
, and extracting from this field,
and also from the operator
\Delta{}Q
(
E.2
), the ghost coordinates
and momenta
\eta_{m}
,
\mathcal{P}_{m}^{+}
,
\eta_{ij}
,

\mathcal{P}_{ij}^{+}
,
i\leq j
,
\vartheta_{12}
,
\lambda_{12}^{+}
,
we deduce a set of gauge conditions for the field
|\chi^{0}\rangle
:

\Bigl([\mathcal{A}^{3}],b_{1}\mathcal{P}_{1}^{+}\prod_{i,j=1,i\leq j}^{2}\mathcal{P}_{ij}^{+},b_{2}\prod_{m}^{2}\mathcal{P}_{m}^{+}\prod_{i,j=1,i\leq j}^{2}\mathcal{P}_{ij}^{+},d_{12}\lambda_{12}^{+}\prod_{m}^{2}\mathcal{P}_{m}^{+}\prod_{i,j=1,i\leq j}^{2}\mathcal{P}_{ij}^{+}\Bigr)|\chi^{0}\rangle=0.

(E.17)

Let us now turn to removing the auxiliary fields by using
the equations of motion.

E.2
Removal of auxiliary fields by resolving equations of motion

As a first step, we decompose the field
|S^{0}\rangle
in the form

\displaystyle|S^{0}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|S^{0}_{0}\rangle+\mathcal{P}_{11}^{+}|S^{0}_{1}\rangle,\qquad\qquad|S^{0}_{(0)_{3}}\rangle=|S^{0}_{(0)_{3}0}\rangle+\mathcal{P}_{1}^{+}|S^{0}_{(0)_{3}1}\rangle,

(E.18)

\displaystyle|S^{0}_{0}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|S^{0}_{00}\rangle+\mathcal{P}_{12}^{+}|S^{0}_{01}\rangle,\qquad\quad|S^{0}_{(0)_{3}0}\rangle=|S^{0}_{(0)_{5}}\rangle+\mathcal{P}_{2}^{+}|S^{0}_{(0)_{4}1}\rangle,

\displaystyle|S^{0}_{(0)_{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|S^{0}_{(0)_{2}0}\rangle+\mathcal{P}_{22}^{+}|S^{0}_{(0)_{2}1}\rangle,\qquad|S^{0}_{(0)_{5}}\rangle=|S^{0}_{(0)_{5}0}\rangle+\lambda_{12}^{+}|S^{0}_{(0)_{5}1}\rangle,

and do the same with the vector
|B^{0}\rangle
,

\displaystyle|B^{0}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{11}^{+}|B^{0}_{1}\rangle+\mathcal{P}_{12}^{+}|B^{0}_{01}\rangle+\mathcal{P}_{22}^{+}|B^{0}_{(0)_{2}1}\rangle+\mathcal{P}_{1}^{+}|B^{0}_{(0)_{3}1}\rangle+\mathcal{P}_{2}^{+}|B^{0}_{(0)_{4}1}\rangle+\lambda_{12}^{+}|B^{0}_{(0)_{5}1}\rangle,

where the term independent of the ghost momenta is absent due to
the spin value and the ghost number condition
gh(|B^{0}\rangle)=-1
.
Notice that, due to the spin value and the fact that

gh(|S^{0}\rangle)=0
, the vector
|S^{0}_{(0)_{6}}\rangle
does not
depend on the ghost coordinates and momenta, as a consequence
of the gauge conditions (
E.17
),
|S^{0}_{(0)_{6}}\rangle=|\Phi\rangle
,
with
|\Phi\rangle
being the physical field (
7
) for
k=2
.

Next, similar to the fields, we extract from
\Delta{}Q
(
E.2
)
the dependence on
\eta_{11}
,
\mathcal{P}_{11}^{+}
,
\eta_{12}
,

\mathcal{P}_{12}^{+}
,
\eta_{22}
,
\mathcal{P}_{22}^{+}
, and then
the dependence on
\eta_{l}
,
\mathcal{P}_{l}^{+}
,
l=1,2
,
as well as on
\vartheta_{12}
,
\lambda_{12}^{+}
, respectively.

Substituting these
6
decompositions into the equation of motion

\displaystyle\tilde{L}_{0}|S^{0}\rangle-\Delta Q|B^{0}\rangle=0,

(E.19)

and using the gauge conditions (
E.17
), we can show that

|B^{0}_{(0)_{5}1}\rangle\hskip-1.00006pt=0
, and then

|B^{0}_{(0)_{4}1}\hskip-1.00006pt\rangle
=
0
, etc., until

|B^{0}_{1}\rangle=0
, which implies

\displaystyle\tilde{L}_{0}|S^{0}\rangle=0,

\displaystyle|B^{0}\rangle=0.

(E.20)

In a similar way, we consider the second equation of motion

\displaystyle\Delta Q|S^{0}\rangle=0,

(E.21)

where
|B^{0}\rangle=0
has been used. After the same decomposition,
we deduce, step-by-step, the facts that

\displaystyle|S^{0}_{(0)_{5}1}\rangle=|S^{0}_{(0)_{4}1}\rangle=\ldots=|S^{0}_{01}\rangle=|S^{0}_{1}\rangle=0.

(E.22)

The relations (
E.20
) and (
E.22
) imply that all of the auxiliary fields
are zero, and then, as a result, we have
|\chi^{0}\rangle_{(s)_{k}}=|\Phi\rangle
.
The operator
{\widetilde{L}}_{0}
reduces to
L_{0}
, with no dependence
on the auxiliary oscillators when acting on
|\Phi\rangle
, so that
the equations of motion (
2
), (
3
)–(
5
)
hold true, with allowance for the mass term
m_{0}^{2}
(
104
). Therefore,
we have proved that the space of cohomologies for the BRST operator
Q
(
91
)
with a vanishing ghost number is determined only by the constraints related
to an irreducible AdS group representation.
